) is bounded below by:

\displaystyle\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{2}-\frac{C_{1}}{2}\left(\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{2}+|b_{0}|s_{0}\right)\frac{\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{2}-\frac{C_{1}}{2\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}\left(\frac{\min\{b^{2}_{0},b^{2}_{0}/s^{2}_{0}\}}{2}+\min\{b^{2}_{0}s_{0},b_{0}^{2}\}\right)

(
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{2}\left(1-\frac{3C_{1}}{2\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}\right).

So (
A.22
) will be positive if we set
C_{1,\beta}>9C_{1}^{2}/4
. On the other hand, if:

\displaystyle\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{s_{0}^{2}}\right\}\leq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\implies|t-t_{0}|\geq\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}},\;\forall\;t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}

then (
A.22
) is bounded below by:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}}{2}\left(\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{2}+|b_{0}|s_{0}\right)\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}.

Suppose that
s_{0}^{2}>1
so that
(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\geq b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}
, then:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}}{2\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}\left(\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{2}+s^{2}_{0}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}.

(
s_{0}^{2}\leq\overline{s}^{2}<\infty
)

Suppose that
s_{0}^{2}>\overline{\nu}^{2}>1
for all
T
, then:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}\geq\frac{f(\overline{\nu}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}(\overline{s}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}

(
s_{0}^{2}\leq\overline{s}^{2}<\infty
)

and we can pick
C_{1,\beta}
large enough so that the RHS is positive. If
s_{0}^{2}<\underline{\nu}^{2}
for some
\underline{\nu}^{2}<1
for all
T
, then we can use the same argument and the fact that
s_{0}>\underline{s}>0
to show (
A.22
) is positive for large
C_{1,\beta}
. On the other hand, if
\lim_{T\to\infty}s_{0}^{2}=1
, then:

\displaystyle\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}=\frac{1}{2}

so for large
T
, we will have:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}>-\frac{1}{4}\implies f(s_{0}^{2})>\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{4}

and thus:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}\geq\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}\left(1-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}}{2\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}\right).

So for
C_{1,\beta}>C_{1}^{2}\overline{s}^{2}/4
bound (
A.22
) is positive.

Having established that bound (
A.21
) is increasing as a function of
t-t_{0}
, then (
A.21
) is minimized by setting:

\displaystyle t

\displaystyle=\inf\;\{t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}:t>t_{0}\}

\displaystyle=t_{0}+\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{\max\{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\},(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\}}.

Suppose first that
\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
, then returning to bound (
A.21
), there is some
B_{1}>0
so that:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,\beta}}{2\min\{1,s_{0}^{-2}\}}-\left(\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{2\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}}+\frac{s_{0}}{\min\{1,s_{0}^{-1}\}}\right)C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}\right]\log T-B_{1}\log T

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{\max\{1,s_{0}^{2}\}(C_{1,\beta}-3C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}})}{2}-B_{1}\right]\log T.

(
|s_{0}^{2}-1|\leq\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}
)

So if we set
C_{1,\beta}>\max\{(3C_{1}+1)^{2},4(C_{\pi}+B_{1})^{2}\}
, then (
A.15
) holds. On the other hand, if
\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}\leq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
, then:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,\beta}f(s_{0}^{2})}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{|b_{0}|s_{0}}{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}\right)C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}\right]\log T-B_{1}\log T

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,\beta}f(s_{0}^{2})}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\frac{3\max\{s_{0}^{2},1\}C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}{2}-B_{1}\right]\log T.

(
|s^{2}_{0}-1|\geq\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}
)

Again, if
s_{0}^{2}>\overline{\nu}^{2}>1
for all
T
, then:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,\beta}f(\overline{\nu}^{2})}{2(\overline{s}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\frac{3\overline{s}^{2}C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}{2}-B_{1}\right]\log T

and we can pick
C_{1,\beta}
large enough so that the term in square brackets is greater than
C_{\pi}
. An identical argument gives the same result when
s_{0}^{2}<\underline{\nu}^{2}
for some
\underline{\nu}<1
. When
\lim_{T\to\infty}s_{0}^{2}=1
, then again for large
T
, we will have:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}\to\frac{1}{2}>-\frac{1}{4}\implies\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}>\frac{1}{4}

and
\max\{s_{0}^{2},1\}<3/2
, so:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,\beta}}{8}-\frac{9C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}{4}-B_{1}\right]\log T

and again we can pick
C_{1,\beta}
large enough so that the term in square brackets is greater than
C_{\pi}
.

Case 2:
t<t_{0}
and
t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}
.

From (
A.16
) we again have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle=\log\left(\frac{T-t+1}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}+\frac{(t_{0}-t)}{2}

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>-\left(\frac{T-t_{0}+1}{2}\right)\log\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t_{0}+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(1).

After standardizing terms we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}

\displaystyle=(t_{0}-t)+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1),

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t_{0}+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=s_{0}^{2}\left[1+\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t_{0}+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)^{2}\right].

Therefore, we can write:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>\log\left(\frac{T-t+1}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>-\left(\frac{T-t_{0}+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t_{0}+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\mathcal{O}(1).

By the same argument we used to show (
A.18
), with probability one we have:

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{T-t_{0}+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t_{0}+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)^{2}\right]\geq-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\frac{1}{2(T-t_{0}+1)}\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}

and since
t<t_{0}
implies
\log\left(\frac{T-t+1}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)>0
, we have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\frac{1}{2(T-t_{0}+1)}\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\mathcal{O}(1).

(A.23)

For notational convenience, we can define the error term:

\displaystyle\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}+s_{0}^{2}\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]-\frac{2s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}

(A.24)

then we can write:

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(s_{0}z_{t^{\prime}}+b_{0})^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(s_{0}z_{t^{\prime}}+b_{0})}{T-t+1}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}+s_{0}^{2}\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{2}{(T-t+1)^{2}}\left[(t_{0}-t)b_{0}s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}-s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}-(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle=1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{2}{(T-t+1)^{2}}\left[(t_{0}-t)b_{0}s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}-(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right]+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})

On
\mathcal{E}
, for some constant
C_{1}>0
we have:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}

(on
\mathcal{E}_{1}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

\displaystyle\left|b_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}}{4}

(on
\mathcal{E}_{8}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

and thus:

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq 1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})

Returning to bound (
A.23
), we now have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\frac{1}{2(T-t_{0}+1)}\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(1).

(A.25)

We now treat the case of
t_{0}-t\geq T-t_{0}+1
and
t_{0}-t<T-t_{0}+1
separately.

Case 2.a:
t_{0}-t\leq T-t_{0}+1
.

We can start by rewriting bound (
A.25
) as:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\frac{(t_{0}-t)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\frac{1}{2(T-t_{0}+1)}\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(1).

(A.26)

Note that if
s_{0}^{2}=1
, then:

\displaystyle s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})=\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}

and on
\mathcal{E}
:

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2},

(on
\mathcal{E}_{3}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4},

(on
\mathcal{E}_{6}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

so
\left|s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|\leq 1/2
. On the other hand, if
s_{0}^{2}\neq 1
, then since
(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
, Assumption
2
implies
T-t_{0}+1\gg\log T
. On
\mathcal{E}
we have:

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)\right|,\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|,\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{(T-t+1)\log T}

(on
\mathcal{E}_{1}\cap\mathcal{E}_{2}\cap\mathcal{E}_{7}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

Additionally since
T-t+1\geq T-t_{0}+1\gtrsim\log T
, on
\mathcal{E}_{5}
for large for some
C_{2}>C_{1}
we can write:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)\right|\leq C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-1)\log T}+C_{1}\log T

\displaystyle\leq C_{2}\sqrt{(T-t+1)\log T}.

(
T-t+1\geq t_{0}-t
and
T-t+1\gtrsim\log T
)

Then on
\mathcal{E}
:

\displaystyle\left|s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(1+s_{0}^{-2}+2s_{0}^{-1})\left[\frac{C_{2}\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{T-t+1}}+\frac{C_{2}^{2}\log T}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 8\underline{s}^{2}C_{2}^{2}\left[\frac{\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{T-t_{0}+1}}+\frac{\log T}{T-t_{0}+1}\right].

(
s_{0}^{2}>\underline{s}^{2}
and
T-t+1\geq T-t_{0}+1
)

So
\left|s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|\to 0
and we can use the inequality
\log(1+x)>x/(x+1)
for
x>-1
to write:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\left[s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})-\frac{s_{0}^{-4}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})^{2}}{1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2s_{0}^{2}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{2s_{0}^{2}(T-t+1)}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{2(T-t+1)}

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\;-\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}}{s_{0}(T-t+1)}-\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\frac{s_{0}^{-4}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})^{2}}{1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}.

We showed above that if
s_{0}^{2}=1
, then
\left|s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|\leq 1/2
on
\mathcal{E}
. Furthermore, since:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle<C_{1}^{1/2}(T-t+1)^{3/4}\log^{1/4}T

(on
\mathcal{E}_{4}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)\right|

\displaystyle<C_{1}\sqrt{(T-t+1)\log T},

(on
\mathcal{E}_{7}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

then for this case on
\mathcal{E}
:

\displaystyle s_{0}^{-4}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})^{2}=\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4C_{1}^{2}\log T}{T-t+1}

and thus:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\frac{s_{0}^{-4}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})^{2}}{1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{1}^{2}\log T.

On the other hand, when
s_{0}^{2}\neq 1
, we showed above that on
\mathcal{E}
:

\displaystyle\left|s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq 16\underline{s}^{2}C_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log T}{T-t+1}}\to 0.

So for
T
large enough that
16\underline{s}^{2}C_{2}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log T}{T-t+1}}\leq 1/2
, we have:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\frac{s_{0}^{-4}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})^{2}}{1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}

\displaystyle\leq(16\underline{s}^{2}C_{2}^{2})^{2}\log T.

On
\mathcal{E}
we also have:

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{2s_{0}^{2}(T-t+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}^{2}}{2\underline{s}^{2}}\log T

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}}{s_{0}(T-t+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}^{2}}{\underline{s}}\log T

and, since
T-t+1\geq T-t_{0}+1
, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{2(T-t_{0}+1)}-\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{2(T-t+1)}\geq 0

so returning to bound (
A.26
) we have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>\frac{(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\frac{(t_{0}-t)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

(A.27)

Next we can write:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1-\left(\frac{s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}{1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right)\left(\frac{\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}}\right)\right]

and have just shown that:

\displaystyle\frac{s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}{1+s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{T-t_{0}+1}}\right),

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right)=\mathcal{O}(1).

(
s_{0}^{-2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
)

Note that if
s_{0}^{2}=1
, then we can completely ignore the first line of (
A.27
), on the other hand, if
s_{0}^{2}\neq 1
, then we showed above that
\left|s_{0}^{-2}\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|\to 0
on
\mathcal{E}
, so for some constant
C_{3}>0
, a first order Taylor approximation of
\log(1+x)
around zero gives:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1-\left(\frac{\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right)\left(\frac{\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t_{0}+1}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\log T}{T-t_{0}+1}}\right)+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

\displaystyle=(s_{0}^{-2}-1)\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}\right)+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

(
t_{0}-t\leq T-t_{0}+1
)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{C_{3}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|}{2}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

We also have:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)\right|<C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}+\log T

(on
\mathcal{E}_{5}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

and since
C_{1}>C_{3}
and
C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\log T\leq C_{1}(\underline{s}^{-2}+1)\log T
WLOG, then we can now lower bound (
A.27
) with:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}+\frac{(t_{0}-t)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

(A.28)

We now split our analysis into the case of
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
and
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}<(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
. However in both cases, we are assuming that
\max\{\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\},(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\}>0
(i.e.
\mathcal{T}_{\beta}
is non-empty for large enough
T
), and when
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}>0
we assume Assumption
1
holds, while when
(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}>0
we assume Assumption
2
holds, so it is without loss of generality to assume Assumption
1
holds when
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
and that Assumption
2
holds when
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}<(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
.

Case 2.a.i:
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}

Suppose that:

\displaystyle\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{s_{0}^{2}}\right\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}

\displaystyle\implies|t-t_{0}|\geq\frac{C_{a,1}\log T}{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}},\;\forall\;t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}.

For
C_{1,\beta}>64C_{1}^{2}
, we can write:

\displaystyle\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}\left[\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2C_{1}\sqrt{\log T}}{|b_{0}|\sqrt{t_{0}-t}}\right]

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}\left[\frac{1}{2}-\frac{2C_{1}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}\right]

(
|t-t_{0}|\geq\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}}
)

\displaystyle>\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{4(T-t+1)^{2}}.

(
C_{1,\beta}>64C_{1}^{2}
)

Furthermore, we have shown that
\left|\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|\leq 1
for large
T
on
\mathcal{E}
, and we have:

\displaystyle-1<\underline{s}^{2}-1<\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}<\overline{s}^{2}-1<\infty

so we can replace (
A.28
) with the deterministic bound:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}+\frac{(t_{0}-t)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

Note that the term:

\displaystyle\frac{(t_{0}-t)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

is decreasing in
t
and is clearly equal to zero when
t=t_{0}
. To see this we can take the derivative with respect
t
to get:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left[\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}-\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]\right]

\displaystyle\leq 0.

(
\log(1+x)>x/(1+x)
for
x>-1
)

Therefore:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

(A.29)

Suppose first that
\lim_{T\to\infty}b_{0}^{2}=0
, then for large
T
:

\displaystyle\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}=0\implies\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)^{2}b_{0}^{4}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}.

So for large
T
, we can lower bound (
A.29
) in this case with:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{64\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

\displaystyle\geq(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}\left[\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{64\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}-\frac{C_{1}s_{0}^{-2}\min\{1,s_{0}^{-1}\}\sqrt{\log T}}{|b_{0}|\sqrt{t_{0}-t}}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

(
(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\leq\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}
)

\displaystyle\geq(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}\left[\frac{1}{128\overline{s}^{2}}-\frac{C_{1}\underline{s}^{-2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

(
|t_{0}-t|\geq\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log^{2}T}{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}}
)

so for
C_{1,\beta}>(128C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}/\underline{s}^{2})^{2}
and some
B_{1}>0
, we have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

\displaystyle\geq(C_{1,\beta}-B_{1})\log T

(
|t_{0}-t|\geq\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}}
)

and thus (
A.15
) holds for
C_{1,\beta}>B_{1}+C_{\pi}
.

Next, if
b_{0}^{2}>\underline{b}^{2}
for some
\underline{b}^{2}>0
, then we can show that the bound (
A.29
) is decreasing in
t
. We verify below that the second term is decreasing in
t
, and the first term disappears when
s_{0}^{2}=1
, so it is without loss of generality to assume that
s_{0}^{2}\neq 1
. Taking the derivative with respect to
t
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left[\frac{C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{t_{0}-t}}-\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{8\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}}\right]

(A.30)

Suppose that
b_{0}^{2}\to\infty
as
T\to\infty
, then since we can write:

\displaystyle-\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{8\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}}\leq-\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)^{2}b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}}

(
\log(1+x)\geq x/(1+x)
for
x>-1
)

and:

\displaystyle\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)^{2}b_{0}^{2}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}=\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{\left(1+\frac{t_{0}-t}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)^{2}}\geq\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{4}.

(
t_{0}-t\leq T-t_{0}+1
)

Then for large
T
:

\displaystyle-\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{8\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}}\lesssim-\frac{T-t_{0}+1}{t_{0}-t}.

At the same time we have:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{T-t_{0}+1}{t_{0}-t}}{\frac{C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{t_{0}-t}}}\geq\frac{\sqrt{T-t_{0}+1}}{C_{1}|\underline{s}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{\log T}}.

(
t_{0}-t\leq T-t_{0}+1
)

Since
s_{0}^{2}\neq 0
, then again
T-t_{0}+1\gg\log T
and the lower bound above is diverging as
T\to\infty
, and thus the first term in (
A.30
) is dominated by the remaining terms and we get that (
A.30
) is negative for large
T
.

On the other hand, if
b_{0}^{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
so that
b_{0}^{2}<\overline{b}^{2}
for some
\overline{b}^{2}<\infty
, then since the first term of (
A.30
) is decreasing in
t
, we have:

\displaystyle\frac{C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{t_{0}-t}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}\sqrt{\log T}}{s_{0}^{2}\sqrt{t_{0}-t}}

(
(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\leq\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}
)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}\sqrt{\log T}}{s_{0}^{2}\sqrt{t_{0}-t}}\bigg|_{t=\sup\{t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}:t\leq t_{0},\;t_{0}-t\leq T-t_{0}+1\}}

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{1}\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s^{2}_{0}\}}{s_{0}^{2}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\overline{b}^{2}}{\underline{s}^{4}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}

(
|b_{0}|\leq\overline{b}<\infty
)

while defining:

\displaystyle h(t):=\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}

and taking the derivative of the other terms with respect to
t
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{h^{\prime\prime}(t)}{1+\frac{h(t)}{T-t+1}}-\frac{\left[\frac{h(t)}{T-t+1}+h^{\prime}(t)\right]^{2}}{(T-t+1)\left[1+\frac{h(t)}{T-t+1}\right]^{2}}.

Since:

\displaystyle h^{\prime\prime}(t)

\displaystyle=-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)^{2}b_{0}^{2}}{8\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{3}}<0

then:

\displaystyle-\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{8\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq-\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{8\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}}\bigg|_{t=\inf\{t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}:t\leq t_{0},\;t_{0}-t\leq T-t_{0}+1\}}

\displaystyle=-\log\left[1+\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}}\right]+\frac{\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}}}{1+\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}}}

\displaystyle\leq-\log\left[1+\frac{\underline{b}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}}\right]+\frac{\frac{\underline{b}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}}}{1+\frac{\underline{b}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}}}

(
0<\underline{b}<|b_{0}|
)

Once more,
\log(1+x)>x/(1+x)
for
x>-1
, and since the last bound above is fixed with respect to
T
, then there is some
b^{*}>0
so that:

\displaystyle-\log\left[1+\frac{\underline{b}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}}\right]+\frac{\frac{\underline{b}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}}}{1+\frac{\underline{b}^{2}}{32\overline{s}^{2}}}<-b^{*}.

So if
C_{1,\beta}>(C_{1}\overline{b}^{2}/\underline{s}^{4}b^{*})^{2}
, then (
A.30
) is negative and the bound (
A.29
) is minimized at:

\displaystyle t

\displaystyle=\sup\;\{t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}:t<t_{0}\}

\displaystyle\leq t_{0}-\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}}.

and thus:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-\frac{C_{1}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}\log T}{s_{0}^{2}\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}}

\displaystyle\quad\>+\frac{T-t_{0}+1}{2}\left(1+\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}(T-t_{0}+1)}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t_{0}+1)\min\{1,s_{0}^{-2}\}}}{\left(1+\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}(T-t_{0}+1)}\right)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

By Assumption
1
we have:

\displaystyle\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}(T-t_{0}+1)

\displaystyle\gg\log T

so for any
C_{1,\beta}>0
, for large
T
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}(T-t_{0}+1)}\leq\sqrt{2}-1

and:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-\frac{C_{1}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}\log T}{s_{0}^{2}\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}}+\frac{T-t_{0}+1}{2}\log\left[1+\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{32\overline{s}^{2}(T-t_{0}+1)}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

Suppose that
b_{0}^{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
, then it must be the case that
T-t_{0}+1\gg\log T
, in which case we can use another first order Taylor approximation to write:

\displaystyle\frac{T-t_{0}+1}{2}\log\left[1+\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{32\overline{s}^{2}(T-t_{0}+1)}\right]=\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{32\overline{s}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\log T\right)

and since
(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\leq\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}
, for some
B_{1}>0
we have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}\log T}{\underline{s}^{2}}+\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{32\overline{s}^{2}}-B_{1}\log T

so we can select
C_{1,\beta}>0
large so that (
A.15
) holds. On the other hand, if
b_{0}^{2}\to\infty
, then by Assumption
1
:

\displaystyle T-t_{0}+1\gtrsim\log T

so we can write:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}(\underline{s}^{-2}+1)\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}\log T}{|b_{0}|}+\frac{B_{2}\log T}{2}\log\left[1+\frac{C_{1,\beta}}{32\overline{s}^{2}B_{2}}\right]-B_{1}\log T

for some
B_{2}>0
. Since
|b_{0}|\to\infty
, for large
T
we

\displaystyle\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}(\underline{s}^{-2}+1)}{|b_{0}|}\leq B_{1}

and clearly we can pick
C_{1,\beta}>0
large so that:

\displaystyle\log\left[1+\frac{C_{1,\beta}}{32\overline{s}^{2}B_{2}}\right]>2B_{1}+C_{\pi}

so again (
A.15
) holds for this case.

Case 2.a.ii:
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}\leq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}

Returning to bound (
A.28
), we can write:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}+\frac{(t_{0}-t)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1-\frac{\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

(A.31)

Since
(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
by assumption, then again Assumption
2
implies
T-t_{0}+1\gg\log T
. We have shown that on
\mathcal{E}
:

\displaystyle\left|\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(1+s_{0}^{2}+2s_{0})\left[\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{T-t+1}}+\frac{C_{1}^{2}\log T}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4\overline{s}^{2}\left[\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{T-t_{0}+1}}+\frac{C_{1}^{2}\log T}{T-t_{0}+1}\right]

so for large
T
we have
\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})>-\underline{s}^{2}/2
, and we have:

\displaystyle-1<\underline{s}^{2}-1<\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}<\overline{s}^{2}-1<\infty

so:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{1}|s_{0}^{-2}-1|\sqrt{\log T(t_{0}-t)}+\frac{(t_{0}-t)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1-\frac{4(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{\underline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

(A.32)

Next, since
(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
and
\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}\leq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
, then then there is some
\overline{b}>0
so that:

\displaystyle\frac{4(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{\underline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\leq\frac{4\overline{b}C_{1}\sqrt{\log T}}{\underline{s}^{2}\sqrt{T-t_{0}+1}}.

(
T-t+1\geq T-t_{0}+1\geq t_{0}-t
)

Since
T-t_{0}+1\gg\log T
in this case, then the RHS above is converging to zero and we can use another first order Taylor approximation to write:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1-\frac{4(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{\underline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{4(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{\underline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}+\mathcal{O}\left(\log T\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{4\overline{s}C_{1}|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{\underline{s}^{2}}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}+\mathcal{O}\left(\log T\right).

(
\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}\leq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
)

So for some
C_{2}>8\overline{s}C_{1}/\underline{s}^{2}
, we can write:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{2}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}+\frac{(t_{0}-t)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(t_{0}-t)(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

\displaystyle=h_{T}(t,t_{0})

We now show that for any
t_{0}
,
h_{T}
is decreasing in
t
. Analyzing the first derivative of
h_{T}
we have:

\displaystyle\frac{\partial h_{T}}{\partial t}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left[C_{2}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{\frac{\log T}{t_{0}-t}}-\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)(T-t_{0}+1)}{T-t+1}\right]+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)(T-t_{0}+1)}{T-t+1}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left[C_{2}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{\frac{\log}{(T-t_{0}+1)x}}-\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{1+x}\right]+\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{1+x}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{1+x}}\right]

(
x=\frac{t_{0}-t}{T-t_{0}+1}
)

Next, note that the term:

\displaystyle g_{1}(x):=C_{2}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{\frac{\log T}{(T-t_{0}+1)x}}

is convex and decreasing as a function of
x
. On the other hand, if we define:

\displaystyle g_{2}(x)

\displaystyle:=-\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{1+x}\right]+\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{1+x}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{1+x}}

then we have:

\displaystyle\frac{dg_{2}}{dx}=\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{(1+x)^{3}}}{\left(1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{1+x}\right)^{2}}>0

so this term is increasing as a function of
x
. Therefore:

\displaystyle g_{1}(x)+g_{2}(x)

\displaystyle\leq g_{1}(x)\bigg|_{x=\inf\>\{x\in[0,1]\>:\>t_{0}-t\leq T-t_{0}+1,\;t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}\}}+g_{2}(x)\bigg|_{x=\sup\>\{x\in[0,1]\>:\>t_{0}-t\leq T-t_{0}+1,\;t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}\}}

\displaystyle\leq g_{1}\left(\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}\right)+g_{2}\left(1\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}-\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}\right]+\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}.

Taking the derivative of the second and third term with respect to
s_{0}
gives:

\displaystyle-\frac{s_{0}(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2\left(1+\frac{s_{0}^{2}-1}{2}\right)^{2}}

(A.33)

Suppose that there is some
\overline{\nu}>1
so that
s_{0}^{2}>\overline{\nu}^{2}
for all
T
, then (
A.33
) is negative, implying:

\displaystyle\frac{C_{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}-\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}\right]+\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}(\overline{s}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}-\log\left[1+\frac{(\overline{\nu}^{2}-1)}{2}\right]+\frac{\frac{(\overline{\nu}^{2}-1)}{2}}{1+\frac{(\overline{\nu}^{2}-1)}{2}}

\displaystyle:=\frac{C_{2}(\overline{s}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}-\nu^{*}.

The inequality
\log(1+x)>x/(x+1)
for
x>-1
and the fact that
\overline{\nu}^{2}>1
imply that
\nu^{*}>0
, so if we pick
C_{1,\beta}>(C_{1}(\overline{s}^{2}-1)^{2}/\nu^{*})^{2}
, then we will have
\partial h_{T}/\partial t<0
. On the other hand, if there is some
\underline{\nu}<1
so that
s_{0}^{2}<\overline{\nu}^{2}
for all
T
, then (
A.33
) is positive, implying:

\displaystyle\frac{C_{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}-\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}\right]+\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}(\overline{s}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}-\log\left[1+\frac{(\underline{\nu}^{2}-1)}{2}\right]+\frac{\frac{(\underline{\nu}^{2}-1)}{2}}{1+\frac{(\underline{\nu}^{2}-1)}{2}}

and we can use the same argument to pick
C_{1,\beta}>0
large enough so that
\partial h_{T}/\partial t<0
. On the other hand, if
\lim_{T\to\infty}s_{0}^{2}=1
, we can again use the inequality
\log(1+x)>x/(1+x)
for
x>-1
to write:

\displaystyle-\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}\right]+\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}\leq 0

and at the same time, a first order Taylor approximation of
\log(1+x)
gives:

\displaystyle-\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}\right]+\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}+\mathcal{O}\left((s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\right)-\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left((s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\right)+\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{4}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left((s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\right)

so this term is negative and converges to zero at rate
\mathcal{O}((s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2})
. Therefore, there is some
B_{1}>0
such that:

\displaystyle\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{\frac{C_{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}}{\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}\right]-\frac{\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}{1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}}}=\frac{B_{1}}{\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}}.

So if we pick
C_{1,\beta}>B_{1}^{2}
, then for large
T
we will also have
\partial h_{T}/\partial t<0
in this case, confirming that
h_{T}(t,t_{0})
is decreasing in
t
, and thus:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>h_{T}(t,t_{0})\big|_{t=\sup\;\{t\in\mathcal{T}_{\beta}:t\leq t_{0}\}}

\displaystyle\geq h_{T}\left(t_{0}-\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}},t_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=-C_{2}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}\log T+\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log s_{0}^{2}}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}\log T

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{C_{1,\beta}\log T}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{C_{1,\beta}\log T}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

By Assumption
2
:

\displaystyle\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}=0\implies\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{C_{1,\beta}\log T}}=0.

Therefore, another first order Taylor approximation gives:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{C_{1,\beta}\log T}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}\right)\left(1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{C_{1,\beta}\log T}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{C_{1,\beta}\log T}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{1,\beta}(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}\log T+\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{C_{1,\beta}^{2}\log^{2}T}{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)+C_{1,\beta}\log T}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{C_{1,\beta}(s_{0}^{-2}-1)}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}\log T+\mathcal{O}\left(\log T\right)

(
(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\gg\log T
)

and thus for some
B_{1}>0
:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>\left(\frac{C_{1,\beta}f(s_{0}^{-2})}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-C_{2}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}-B_{1}\right)\log T

Once again, if
s_{0}^{2}\geq\overline{\nu}^{2}>1
for all
T
, then
f(s_{0}^{-2})/(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\geq f(\overline{\nu}^{-2})/(\overline{s}^{2}-1)^{2}>0
, while if
s_{0}^{2}\leq\underline{\nu}^{2}<1
, then
f(s_{0}^{-2})/(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\geq f(\underline{\nu}^{-2})>0
, and since:

\displaystyle\lim_{s_{0}^{2}\to 1}\frac{f(s_{0}^{-2})}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}=\frac{1}{2}

then if
\lim_{T\to\infty}s_{0}^{2}=1
, then for large enough
T
,
f(s_{0}^{-2})/(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}>1/4
, so again we can pick
C_{1,\beta}>0
large enough that:

\displaystyle\frac{C_{1,\beta}f(s_{0}^{-2})}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-C_{2}\sqrt{C_{1,\beta}}-B_{1}>C_{\pi}

proving (
A.15
) holds for this case.

Case 2.b:
t_{0}-t\leq T-t_{0}+1
.

By the same argument we used in Case 2.a, on
\mathcal{E}
we have
|\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})|>-1
and we can use the inequality
\log(1+x)>x/(x+1)
for
x>-1
to write:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right]

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\left[\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})-\frac{\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})^{2}}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{2(T-t+1)}+\frac{s_{0}^{2}}{2}\left[\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{(T-t+1)}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\;-\frac{s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}}{(T-t+1)}-\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\frac{\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})^{2}}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\frac{s_{0}^{2}}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

Returning to bound (
A.25
), we now have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

(A.34)

As in Case 2.a, we can completely ignore the first line of (
A.34
) if
s_{0}^{2}=1
, and if
s_{0}^{2}\neq 1
, then Assumption
2
implies
T-t_{0}+1\gg\log T
. We have shown that on
\mathcal{E}
:

\displaystyle\left|\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|

\displaystyle\leq(1+s_{0}^{2}+2s_{0})\left[\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{\log T}x}{\sqrt{T-t+1}}+\frac{C_{1}^{2}\log T}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4\overline{s}^{2}\left[\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{T-t_{0}+1}}+\frac{C_{1}^{2}\log T}{T-t_{0}+1}\right]

(
T-t+1\geq T-t_{0}+1
)

so again,
\left|\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right|\to 0
on
\mathcal{E}
when
s_{0}^{2}\neq 1
. Next, we can write:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 8.50012pt\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1-\left(\frac{\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right)\left(\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}\right)\right].

We have just shown that:

\displaystyle\frac{\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{T-t+1}}\right)=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log T}}{\sqrt{T-t_{0}+1}}\right)

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}\left(\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)(T-t_{0}+1)}{T-t+1}\right)=\mathcal{O}(1)

so another first order Taylor approximation of
\log(1+x)
around zero gives:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1-\left(\frac{\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right)\left(\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=(s_{0}^{2}-1)\mathcal{O}\left(\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}\right)+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

Similarly, for some
C_{1}>0
on
\mathcal{E}
we have:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)\right|\leq C_{1}\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}

(on
\mathcal{E}_{7}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

So for some
C_{2}\geq C_{1}/2
, we can now rewrite (
A.34
) on
\mathcal{E}
as:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{2}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

(A.35)

Case 2.b.i:
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}

As in case 2.a.i, we can assume that Assumption
1
holds in this case. Using the same argument from Case 2.a.i, it is straight forward to show that the term:

\displaystyle-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

is decreasing in
t
and equal to zero when
t=t_{0}
, and that on
\mathcal{E}
:

\displaystyle\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]\geq\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right].

So in this case we can write:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{2}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

The second term is decreasing in
t
and
t_{0}-t\geq T-t_{0}+1
implies
t\leq 2t_{0}-T-1
in this case so for some
B_{1}>0
:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{2}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+(T-t_{0}+1)\log\left[1+\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{16^{2}\overline{s}^{2}}\right]-B_{1}\log T.

Next we have:

\displaystyle-C_{2}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+(T-t_{0}+1)\log\left[1+\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{16^{2}\overline{s}^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\geq(T-t_{0}+1)\left(\log\left[1+\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{16^{2}\overline{s}^{2}}\right]-C_{2}(\overline{s}^{2}+1)\sqrt{\frac{\log T}{T-t_{0}+1}}\right).

Suppose that
b_{0}^{2}\to\infty
as
T\to\infty
, then since
T-t_{0}+1\gtrsim\log T
, there is some
B_{2}>0
so that:

\displaystyle\frac{\log T}{B_{2}}\left(\log\left[1+\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{16\overline{s}^{2}}\right]-\frac{C_{2}(\overline{s}^{2}+1)}{\sqrt{B_{2}}}\right)

So (
A.15
) holds for
T
large enough that:

\displaystyle\log\left[1+\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{16^{2}\overline{s}^{2}}\right]>\frac{C_{2}(\overline{s}^{2}+1)}{\sqrt{B_{2}}}+B_{2}(B_{1}+C_{\pi}).

On the other hand, if
b_{0}^{2}=\mathcal{O}(1)
, then Assumption
1
implies
T-t_{0}+1\gg\log T
. If
b_{0}^{2}>\underline{b}^{2}>0
for all
T
, then for large
T
we have:

\displaystyle\log\left[1+\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{16^{2}\overline{s}^{2}}\right]-C_{2}(\overline{s}^{2}+1)\sqrt{\frac{\log T}{T-t_{0}+1}}>\frac{1}{2}\log\left[1+\frac{\underline{b}^{2}}{16^{2}\overline{s}^{2}}\right]>0

and:

\displaystyle\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log\left[1+\frac{\underline{b}^{2}}{16^{2}\overline{s}^{2}}\right]-\log T\geq C_{\pi}\log T.

Finally, if
\lim_{T\to\infty}b_{0}^{2}=0
, then a first order Taylor expansion of
\log(1+x)
gives:

\displaystyle\log\left[1+\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{16^{2}\overline{s}^{2}}\right]=\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{16^{2}\overline{s}^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(b_{0}^{4})\geq\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{512\overline{s}^{2}}

where the last inequality holds for large
T
, and thus:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{2}(\overline{s}^{2}+1)\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}}{512\overline{s}^{2}}-B_{1}\log T.

But Assumption
1
implies
(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}\gg\log T
, so the second term dominates for large
T
we again get that (
A.15
) holds for this case.

Case 2.b.ii:
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}<(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}

As in case 2.a.ii, we can assume that Assumption
2
holds in this case. Returning to bound (
A.35
), we can use the same argument from Case 2.a.ii to get:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{2}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1-\frac{4(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{\underline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

and:

\displaystyle\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1-\frac{4(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{\underline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=-\frac{4(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{(t_{0}-t)\log T}}{\underline{s}^{2}(T-t+1)}+\mathcal{O}\left(\log T\right)

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{4\overline{s}C_{1}|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{\underline{s}^{2}}\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+\mathcal{O}\left(\log T\right).

(
T-t+1\geq t_{0}-t\geq T-t_{0}+1
and
\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}\leq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
)

So for
C_{3}>C_{2}+4\overline{s}C_{1}/\underline{s}^{2}
we have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{3}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\mathcal{O}(\log T).

(A.36)

The bound (
A.36
) is decreasing as a function of
t
and in this case, since
t_{0}-t>T-t_{0}+1
, then
t<2t_{0}-T+1
. Therefore, we have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{3}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+(T-t_{0}+1)\log\left[1+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

\displaystyle=-C_{3}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+(T-t_{0}+1)\log\left[1+\frac{s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2}{2}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

If there is some
\underline{\nu}<1
so that
s_{0}\leq\underline{\nu}
for all
T
, then:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{3}\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+(T-t_{0}+1)\log\left[1+\frac{\underline{\nu}+\underline{\nu}^{-1}-2}{2}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

and if there is some
\overline{\nu}>1
so that
s_{0}\geq\underline{\nu}
for all
T
, then:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{3}|\overline{s}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+(T-t_{0}+1)\log\left[1+\frac{\overline{\nu}+\overline{\nu}^{-1}-2}{2}\right]+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

and since
\min\{\underline{\nu}+\underline{\nu}^{-1},\overline{\nu}+\overline{\nu}^{-1}\}-2>0
and
T-t_{0}+1\gg\log T
, the second term dominates in both the bounds above and for large enough
T
we will have
\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}\geq C_{\pi}\log^{2}T
. On the other hand, if
\lim_{T\to\infty}s_{0}^{2}=1
, then we can use a first order Taylor approximation of
\log(1+x)
around zero to write:

\displaystyle\log\left[1+\frac{s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2}{2}\right]=\frac{s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2}{2}+\mathcal{O}\left(\left(s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2\right)^{2}\right).

Since
\lim_{s_{0}\to 1}s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2=0
, then for large enough
T
, we have:

\displaystyle\log\left[1+\frac{s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2}{2}\right]=\frac{s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2}{4}

and thus:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{3}|s_{0}^{2}-1|\sqrt{(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+(T-t_{0}+1)\left(\frac{s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2}{4}\right)+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

Next, since:

\displaystyle\lim_{s_{0}\to 1}\frac{s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}=\frac{1}{4}

then for large enough
T
we have:

\displaystyle s_{0}+\frac{1}{s_{0}}-2>\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{8}

and thus:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-C_{3}\sqrt{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}(T-t_{0}+1)\log T}+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{8}+\mathcal{O}(\log T)

By Assumption
2
,
(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\gg\log T
, so again the second term dominates the others and (
A.15
) holds on
\mathcal{E}
for large
T
in this case.

∎

A.7
Theorem
4
Event Bounds

Lemma 6

(Theorem
4
Event Bounds)
.

Let
\{y_{t}\}_{t\geq 1}
be a univariate sequence of random variables satisfying Assumption
3
. Let
\mathbb{E}[y_{t}]=b_{0}\mathbbm{1}\{t\geq t_{0}\}
and
{\text{Var}}(y_{t})=(s_{0}^{2})^{\mathbbm{1}\{t\geq t_{0}\}}
, with
b_{0}\in\mathbb{R}
and
s_{0}>0
so that it is possible that
b_{0}=0
or
s^{2}_{0}=1
. Define the standardized term
z_{t}:=(y_{t}-b_{0}\mathbbm{1}\{t\geq t_{0}\})/s_{0}^{\mathbbm{1}\{t\geq t_{0}\}}
and for any sequence
\{a_{T}\}_{T\geq 1}
such that
a_{T}\to\infty
and constants
C,C_{1,a},C_{2,a},C_{3,a}>0
, define the set:

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}

\displaystyle:=\left\{1\leq t\leq T-C_{2,a}a_{T}\log^{2}T\>:\>|t_{0}-t|>\frac{C_{1,a}a_{T}\log^{2}T}{\max\{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\},(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\}}\right\}

and the events:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\bigcap_{t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=\min\{t_{0},t\}}^{\max\{t_{0},t\}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|<C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}|t_{0}-t|}\log T\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{2}

\displaystyle:=\bigcap_{t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=\min\{t_{0},t\}}^{\max\{t_{0},t\}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)\right|<C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}|t_{0}-t|}\log T\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{3}

\displaystyle:=\bigcap_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|<C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{4}

\displaystyle:=\bigcap_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)\right|<C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T\right\},

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{5}

\displaystyle=\left\{\left|b_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|\leq\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}}{4}\right\},

and the joint event
\mathcal{E}:=\bigcap_{i=1}^{4}\mathcal{E}_{i}
. Then if:

\displaystyle\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}\min\{t_{0},T-t_{0}+1\}\gg a_{T}\log^{2}T

or:

\displaystyle(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\min\{t_{0},T-t_{0}+1\}\gg a_{T}\log^{2}T

then there exist
C,C_{1,a},C_{2,a},C_{3,a}
large enough so that we will have
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E})\geq 1-Ca_{T}^{-1}
.

Proof.

By the union bound
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c})=1-P(\cup_{i=1}^{5}\mathcal{E}^{c}_{i})<1-\sum^{5}_{i=1}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c}_{i})
, so it is sufficient to show that
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c}_{i})\leq\frac{C_{i}}{a_{T}}
for each
i
. First, by Assumption
3
there are stochastic processes
\{z_{0,t}\}_{t\geq 1}
and
\{z_{1,t}\}_{t\geq 1}
such that
z_{0,t}\sim F_{0}
,
z_{1,t}\sim F_{1}
,
\mathbb{E}[z_{0,t}]=\mathbb{E}[z_{1,t}]=0
,
\text{Var}(z_{0,t})=\text{Var}(z_{1,t})=1
,
y_{t}:=z_{0,t}\mathbbm{1}_{\{t<t_{0}\}}+(s_{0}z_{1,t}+b_{0})\mathbbm{1}_{\{t\geq t_{0}\}}
, and:

(i)

\{z_{0,t}\}_{t\geq 1}
and
\{z_{1,t}\}_{t\geq 1}
are
\alpha
-mixing processes with respective coefficients
\{\alpha_{0,k}\}_{k\geq 1}
and
\{\alpha_{1,k}\}_{k\geq 1}
that satisfy
\max\{\alpha_{0,k},\alpha_{1,k}\}\leq e^{-Ck}
for some
C>0
.

(ii)

There exist constants
D_{1},D_{2}>0
such that
\sup_{t\geq 1}\max\{\mathbb{E}\left[|z_{0,t}|^{4+D_{1}}\right],\;\mathbb{E}\left[|z_{1,t}|^{4+D_{1}}\right]\}\leq D_{2}.

Therefore, the conditions of Lemma
2
are met. Next we can write:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{c}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=\min\{t_{0},t\}}^{\max\{t_{0},t\}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}|t_{0}-t|}\log T\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}\>:\>t<t_{0}}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T\right\}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}\>:\>t>t_{0}}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{t-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(t-t_{0})}\log T\right\}\right)

(union bound)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=1}^{t_{0}-1}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T\right\}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=t_{0}+1}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{t-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(t-t_{0})}\log T\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=1}^{t_{0}-1}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{0,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T\right\}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=t_{0}+1}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{t-1}z_{1,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(t-t_{0})}\log T\right\}\right)

(
z_{t^{\prime}}=z_{0,t^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\{t^{\prime}<t_{0}\}+z_{1,t^{\prime}}\mathbbm{1}\{t^{\prime}\geq t_{0}\}
)

Since
\{z_{0,t}\}_{t\geq 1}
and
\{z_{1,t}\}_{t\geq 1}
satisfy the conditions of Lemma
3
, for some
K_{1},K_{2}>0
we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=1}^{t_{0}-1}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{0,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq K_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a_{T}}

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=t_{0}+1}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{t-1}z_{1,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq K_{2}\sqrt{a_{T}(t-t_{0})}\log T\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a_{T}}

So for
C_{3,a}>\max\{K_{1},K_{2}\}
we get
\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{1}^{c}\right)\leq\frac{2}{a_{T}}
. Since
\{z^{2}_{0,t}-1\}_{t\geq 1}
and
\{z^{2}_{1,t}-1\}_{t\geq 1}
also satisfy the conditions of Lemma
3
, an identical argument shows we can pick
C_{3,a}
large enough so that
\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{2}^{c}\right)\leq\frac{2}{a_{T}}
as well.

Next, note that if
t<t_{0}
and:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{0,t^{\prime}}\right|<\frac{C_{3,a}}{2}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T

and:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{1,t^{\prime}}\right|<\frac{C_{3,a}}{2}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t_{0}+1)}\log T

then:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|<\frac{C_{3,a}}{2}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T+\frac{C_{3,a}}{2}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T\leq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T.

(
T-t+1>\max\{t_{0}-t,T-t_{0}+1\}
)

So:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}^{c}_{3}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=1}^{t_{0}-1}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T\right\}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=t_{0}}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{1,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T\right\}\right)

(union bound,
z_{t}=z_{1,t}
for
t\geq t_{0}
)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=1}^{t_{0}-1}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{0,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq\frac{C_{3,a}}{2}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T\right\}\cup\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{1,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq\frac{C_{3,a}}{2}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t_{0}+1)}\log T\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=t_{0}}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{1,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T\right\}\right)

(union bound)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=1}^{t_{0}-1}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{0,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq\frac{C_{3,a}}{2}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T\right\}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{1,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq\frac{C_{3,a}}{2}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t_{0}+1)}\log T\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\>+\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=t_{0}}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{1,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T\right\}\right)

(union bound)

Once again, by Lemma
3
there is some
K_{3}>0
such that:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcup_{t=1}^{T}\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{1,t^{\prime}}\right|\geq K_{3}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{a_{T}}

So for
C_{3,a}\geq 2\max\{K_{1},K_{3}\}
, we have
\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{3}^{c}\right)\leq\frac{3}{a_{T}}
. Again, we can use an identical argument to show that we can pick
C_{3,a}
large enough so that
\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{4}^{c}\right)\leq\frac{3}{a_{T}}
.

Finally note that the event
\mathcal{E}_{5}
holds trivially if
b_{0}=0
, otherwise if
b_{0}\neq 0
then by assumption
b_{0}^{2}(T-t_{0}+1)\gg a_{T}\log T
and for
T
large enough that
|b_{0}|\sqrt{T-t_{0}+1}\geq 4C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}}\log T
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{5}^{c})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|>\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|}{4}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|>C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t_{0}+1)}\log T\right)

(
|b_{0}|\sqrt{T-t_{0}+1}\geq 4C_{3,a}\sqrt{a_{T}}\log T
)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{c}_{3}).

∎

A.8
Proof of Theorem
4

Below we show the proof of Theorem
4
for the meanvar-scp model of Section
2.3
. Proving that Theorem
4
holds for the mean-scp and var-scp models of Sections
2.1
and
2.2
respectively is a straightforward extension since the form of the log-evidence for the meanvar-scp model is essentially a combination of the log-evidence for the mean-scp and var-scp models.

As we did for the proof of Theorem
3
in Appendix
A.6
, for some sequence
\{a_{T}\}_{T\geq 1}
and constants
C_{1,a},C_{2,a}>0
, we define the set:

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}:=\left\{1\leq t\leq T-\lfloor C_{2,a}a_{T}\log^{2}T\rfloor\>:\>|t_{0}-t|>\frac{C_{1,a}a_{T}\log^{2}T}{\max\{\min\{b^{2}_{0},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\},(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\}}\right\}.

We also assumed in Theorem
4
that for large
T
there is some constant
C_{\pi}>0
such that for all
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\pi_{t}}{\pi_{t_{0}}}\leq C_{\pi}\log^{2}T.

(A.37)

Therefore, by the same argument we used in the proof of Theorem
3
, if we let
p(\mathbf{y}_{1:T}\>|\>\gamma=t_{0}\>;\lambda_{0},u_{0},v_{0})
be the marginal likelihood for the meanvar-scp model and we show that under the conditions of Theorem
4
, for any
a_{T}\to\infty
, we can pick
C_{1,a},C_{2,a},C_{3,a}>0
large enough such that:

\displaystyle\lim_{T\to\infty}\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcap_{t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}}\left\{\log\frac{p(\mathbf{y}_{1:T}\>|\>\gamma=t_{0}\>;\lambda_{0},u_{0},v_{0})}{p(\mathbf{y}_{1:T}\>|\>\gamma=t\>;\lambda_{0},u_{0},v_{0})}>C_{\pi}\log^{2}T\right\}\right)=1

(A.38)

then we will have proven the desired result. We now prove that there exists a
C_{1,a},C_{2,a}>0
such that the event in (
A.38
) holds on the event
\mathcal{E}
defined in Lemma
6
.

Proof.

As in the proof of Theorem
3
, it is sufficient to show that
\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}>C_{\pi}\log^{2}T
for large
T
on the event
\mathcal{E}
. To show this we again consider two cases:

Case 1:
t>t_{0}
, and
t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}
.

Using the identical argument we used to get (
A.19
) in the proof of Theorem
3
in Appendix
A.6
and the function
f(x)=x-\log(x)-1
, we can begin from:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\log\left(\frac{T-t+1}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)+\left(\frac{t-t_{0}}{2}\right)\left[b_{0}^{2}+f(s_{0}^{2})\right]+\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{t-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+b_{0}s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{t-1}z_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\quad\>-\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{2(T-t+1)}-\frac{\frac{(T-t+1)}{2}\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]^{2}}{1+\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(1).

(A.39)

On the event
\mathcal{E}
defined in Lemma
6
, for some constant
C_{1}>0
we have:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{t-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}(t-t_{0})}\log T,

(on
\mathcal{E}_{1}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{t-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}(t-t_{0})}\log T

(on
\mathcal{E}_{2}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

and thus:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\log\left(\frac{T-t+1}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)+\left(\frac{t-t_{0}}{2}\right)\left[b_{0}^{2}+f(s_{0}^{2})\right]-\left(\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{2}+|b_{0}|s_{0}\right)C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}(t-t_{0})}\log T

\displaystyle\quad\>-\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{2(T-t+1)}-\frac{\frac{(T-t+1)}{2}\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]^{2}}{1+\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}}+\mathcal{O}(1).

(A.40)

Next, on the event
\mathcal{E}
defined in Lemma
6
, for the same universal constant
C_{1}>0
we have:

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|,\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z^{2}_{t^{\prime}}-1)\right|\;\right\}

\displaystyle<C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}(T-t+1)}\log T

(on
\mathcal{E}_{3}\cap\mathcal{E}_{4}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

and thus:

\displaystyle-\frac{\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}}{2(T-t+1)}>-\frac{C_{1}^{2}}{2}a_{T}\log^{2}T

and:

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle<\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}}\log T}{\sqrt{T-t+1}}+\frac{C_{1}^{2}a_{T}\log^{2}T}{T-t+1}.

Suppose we pick
C_{2,a}>4C_{1}^{2}
, then since
t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}
implies
T-t+1\geq C_{2,a}a_{T}\log^{2}T
, then:

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right|

\displaystyle<\frac{3}{4}

and:

\displaystyle\frac{\frac{(T-t+1)}{2}\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]^{2}}{1+\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle<2(T-t+1)\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle<2(T-t+1)\left[\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}}\log T}{\sqrt{T-t+1}}+\frac{C_{1}^{2}a_{T}\log^{2}T}{T-t+1}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=2\left[C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}}\log T+\frac{C_{1}^{2}a_{T}\log^{2}T}{\sqrt{T-t+1}}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{1}^{2}a_{T}\log^{2}T.

(
t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}\implies T-t+1\geq C_{1}a_{T}\log^{2}T
)

We can now rewrite bound (
A.40
) as:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\left(\frac{t-t_{0}}{2}\right)\left[b_{0}^{2}+f(s_{0}^{2})\right]-\left(\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{2}+|b_{0}|s_{0}\right)C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}(t-t_{0})}\log T+\mathcal{O}(a_{T}\log^{2}T)

(A.41)

where we have absorbed the
\log\left(\frac{T-t+1}{T-t_{0}+1}\right)
term into the
\mathcal{O}(a_{T}\log^{2}T)
term. We now show that bound (
A.41
) is increasing as a function of
t-t_{0}
. Taking the derivative of this bound with respect to
t-t_{0}
yields:

\displaystyle\frac{b_{0}^{2}+f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}}{2}\left(\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{2}+|b_{0}|s_{0}\right)\sqrt{\frac{a_{T}}{t-t_{0}}}\log T.

(A.42)

Note that (
A.42
) is decreasing as a function of
t-t_{0}
. Suppose that:

\displaystyle\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{s_{0}^{2}}\right\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\implies|t-t_{0}|\geq\frac{C_{1,a}a_{T}\log^{2}T}{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}},\;\forall\;t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}

and thus (
A.42
) is bounded below by:

\displaystyle\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{2}-\frac{C_{1}}{2}\left(\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{2}+|b_{0}|s_{0}\right)\frac{\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}}{\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{2}-\frac{C_{1}}{2\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}\left(\frac{\min\{b^{2}_{0},b^{2}_{0}/s^{2}_{0}\}}{2}+\min\{b^{2}_{0}s_{0},b_{0}^{2}\}\right)

(
\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\right\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{2}\left(1-\frac{3C_{1}}{2\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}\right).

So (
A.42
) will be positive if we set
C_{1,a}>9C_{1}^{2}/4
. On the other hand, if:

\displaystyle\min\left\{b_{0}^{2},\frac{b_{0}^{2}}{s_{0}^{2}}\right\}\leq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\implies|t-t_{0}|\geq\frac{C_{1,a}a_{T}\log^{2}T}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}},\;\forall\;t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}

then (
A.42
) is bounded below by:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}}{2}\left(\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{2}+|b_{0}|s_{0}\right)\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}.

Suppose that
s_{0}^{2}>1
so that
(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\geq b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}
, then:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}}{2\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}\left(\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{2}+s^{2}_{0}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}.

(
s_{0}^{2}\leq\overline{s}^{2}<\infty
)

Suppose that
s_{0}^{2}>\overline{\nu}^{2}>1
for all
T
, then:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}\geq\frac{f(\overline{\nu}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}(\overline{s}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}

(
s_{0}^{2}\leq\overline{s}^{2}<\infty
)

and we can pick
C_{1,a}
large enough so that the RHS is positive. If
s_{0}^{2}<\underline{\nu}^{2}
for some
\underline{\nu}^{2}<1
for all
T
, then we can use the same argument and the fact that
s_{0}>\underline{s}>0
to show (
A.42
) is positive for large
C_{1,a}
. On the other hand, if
\lim_{T\to\infty}s_{0}^{2}=1
, then:

\displaystyle\lim_{T\to\infty}\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}=\frac{1}{2}

so for large
T
, we will have:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}>-\frac{1}{4}\implies f(s_{0}^{2})>\frac{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{4}

and thus:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}{\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}\geq\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{2}\left(1-\frac{C_{1}\overline{s}^{2}}{2\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}\right).

So for
C_{1,a}>C_{1}^{2}\overline{s}^{2}/4
bound (
A.42
) is positive.

Having established that bound (
A.41
) is increasing as a function of
t-t_{0}
, then (
A.41
) is minimized by setting:

\displaystyle t

\displaystyle=\inf\;\{t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}:t>t_{0}\}

\displaystyle=t_{0}+\frac{C_{1,a}a_{T}\log^{2}T}{\max\{\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\},(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}\}}.

Suppose first that
\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}\geq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
, then returning to bound (
A.41
), there is some
B_{1}>0
so that:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,a}}{2\min\{1,s_{0}^{-2}\}}-\left(\frac{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}{2\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}}+\frac{s_{0}}{\min\{1,s_{0}^{-1}\}}\right)C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,a}}\right]a_{T}\log^{2}T-B_{1}a_{T}\log^{2}T

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{\max\{1,s_{0}^{2}\}(C_{1,a}-3C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,a}})}{2}-B_{1}\right]a_{T}\log^{2}T.

(
|s_{0}^{2}-1|\leq\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}
)

So if we set
C_{1,a}>\max\{(3C_{1}+1)^{2},4(C_{\pi}+B_{1})^{2}\}
, then (
A.38
) holds. On the other hand, if
\min\{b_{0}^{2},b_{0}^{2}/s_{0}^{2}\}\leq(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}
, then:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,a}f(s_{0}^{2})}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\left(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{|b_{0}|s_{0}}{|s_{0}^{2}-1|}\right)C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,a}}\right]a_{T}\log^{2}T-B_{1}a_{T}\log^{2}T

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,a}f(s_{0}^{2})}{2(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\frac{3\max\{s_{0}^{2},1\}C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}{2}-B_{1}\right]a_{T}\log^{2}T.

(
|s^{2}_{0}-1|\geq\min\{|b_{0}|,|b_{0}|/s_{0}\}
)

Again, if
s_{0}^{2}>\overline{\nu}^{2}>1
for all
T
, then:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,a}f(\overline{\nu}^{2})}{2(\overline{s}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\frac{3\overline{s}^{2}C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}{2}-B_{1}\right]a_{T}\log^{2}T

and we can pick
C_{1,a}
large enough so that the term in square brackets is greater than
C_{\pi}
. An identical argument gives the same result when
s_{0}^{2}<\underline{\nu}^{2}
for some
\underline{\nu}<1
. When
\lim_{T\to\infty}s_{0}^{2}=1
, then again for large
T
, we will have:

\displaystyle\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}\to\frac{1}{2}>-\frac{1}{4}\implies\frac{f(s_{0}^{2})}{(s_{0}^{2}-1)^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}>\frac{1}{4}

and
\max\{s_{0}^{2},1\}<3/2
, so:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle\geq\left[\frac{C_{1,a}}{8}-\frac{9C_{1}\sqrt{C_{1,a}}}{4}-B_{1}\right]a_{T}\log^{2}T

and again we can pick
C_{1,a}
large enough so that the term in square brackets is greater than
C_{\pi}
.

Case 2:
t<t_{0}
, and
t\in\mathcal{T}_{a_{T}}
.

Using the identical argument we used to get (
A.23
) in the proof of Theorem
3
in Appendix
A.6
we can begin from:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\frac{1}{2(T-t_{0}+1)}\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\>+\mathcal{O}(1).

(A.43)

For notational convenience, we can define the error term:

\displaystyle\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})

\displaystyle:=\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}+s_{0}^{2}\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]-\frac{2s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}

(A.44)

then we can write:

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(s_{0}z_{t^{\prime}}+b_{0})^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(s_{0}z_{t^{\prime}}+b_{0})}{T-t+1}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}+s_{0}^{2}\left[\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{2}{(T-t+1)^{2}}\left[(t_{0}-t)b_{0}s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}-s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}-(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right]

\displaystyle=1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{(T-t+1)^{2}}+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\frac{2}{(T-t+1)^{2}}\left[(t_{0}-t)b_{0}s_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}-(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right]+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})

On
\mathcal{E}
, for some constant
C_{1}>0
we have:

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T

(on
\mathcal{E}_{1}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

\displaystyle\left|b_{0}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)b_{0}^{2}}{4}

(on
\mathcal{E}_{5}\subset\mathcal{E}
)

and thus:

\displaystyle\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}^{2}}{T-t+1}-\left(\frac{\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}y_{t^{\prime}}}{T-t+1}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq 1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T}{(T-t+1)^{2}}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})

Returning to bound (
A.43
), we now have:

\displaystyle\log\frac{\alpha_{t_{0}}}{\alpha_{t}}

\displaystyle>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{t_{0}-1}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)-\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)}{2}\log s_{0}^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}}{1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\frac{\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(t_{0}-t)b_{0}^{2}}{2(T-t+1)^{2}}-\frac{2(T-t_{0}+1)|b_{0}|C_{1}\sqrt{a_{T}(t_{0}-t)}\log T}{(T-t+1)^{2}}}{1+\frac{(T-t_{0}+1)(s_{0}^{2}-1)}{T-t+1}+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\>-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}(z_{t^{\prime}}^{2}-1)+\frac{1}{2(T-t_{0}+1)}\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=t_{0}}^{T}z_{t^{\prime}}\right)^{2}+\left(\frac{T-t+1}{2}\right)\log\left[1+\xi(T,t_{0},t,s_{0})\right]
